# Seed 79 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - 12 hours: a gain on seed 77 by the fastest and 32-bit - made past generation 161, which seed 77 never reached (Iteration 565)

`seed 79 12h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16 then experiment carry-check.py --all` at fb29ee303. The chain: done. 45,419 designs, generations 0-177: 26,537 ok, 12,059 stopped after one round, 6,456 screened, 312 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 51 wrong output: apps, 4 died: kernel workload; 3,784 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 257 designs - the report's 257, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 257 (257 inside the box), hypervolume 0.35169 | fastest 0.1389, smallest 14055, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1666. **The stopping rule** (stopping.py, results/stopping-series.md): verdict: seed 79 (at its last generation, 177 - the earlier runs there too) GAINS, by the fastest and the fastest at 32 bits - the hypervolume 0.35169 against 0.35010 (+0.5%); the fastest 0.1378 against 0.1406 (-2.0%, past its threshold); the smallest 14,055 against 14,015 (+0.3%); the least memory 112 KB against 108 KB (+4 KB); the fastest at 32 bits 0.1643 against 0.1702 (-3.5%, past its threshold) the count: 3 run(s) on the method; the earliest stop after seed 81, if every run until then gains nothing

**The extremes**: fastest 82bbc95daa 0.1389, smallest 7753132187 14,055 bytes, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 9a26b5a98d 0.1666.

**One round first**: 12,059 children stopped after one round, 31.1% of those measured; 1315 audited in full, 0 of them on the front.

**The operators**: borrowed 37.8%, crossover 28.1%, mutation 34.1% of the children; the last fifth borrowed 38.2%, crossover 26.2%, mutation 35.6%; the front's entries per child 0.568% (257 of 45,277).

**The JIT designs**: 75 of the front, 25 of its fastest tenth's 25; jitmul 37, jitfuse 17, jitexec 14, jitos 0, jitrp 0.

**The fast end re-measured** (remeasure.json, 95 designs): the fastest ten 1.063 re-measured / in-run, the JIT designs 1.044, the rest 1.016.

**The experiments**: the spread at generation 126 - | build/evolve/db.jsonl | 126 | 19444 | 344 | 0.1435 | 14,364 | 112 | 9.9942e+05 | 0.6089 | 0.1878 |; appbench - **Spearman, in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean: 0.806 over 16 designs.**; the clock test - none; truefronts - none.

**The remote checks** (results/xcheck-seed79.md): dream.lan-prebuilt 24 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.890; dream.lan-prebuilt32 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.869; dream.lan-prebuilt64 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.931; rage.lan-native 16 designs measured on both: Spearman -0.103.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve (held out): 257 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.954 at the top, where selection chooses: the fastest third (85) 0.877, the fastest tenth (25) 0.551 regret: the selection's fastest, 82bbc95daa, 0.069 held out; the held-out fastest, 702166ae66, 0.062 - the pick +11.3%

**The curse** (curse.txt - rule 3, 0.98-1.05 works): fastest tenth    7 designs: median 1.094, range 1.038-1.099 fastest third   26 designs: median 1.073, range 1.038-1.099

**Progress**: the smallest binary + image (exact) gained in 30 generations; the last at generation 176 of 177; in the last third 8 gains, 309 bytes in all the best speed gained 2% or more in 19 generations; the last at generation 146 (41: 4.0%, 42: 3.2%, 47: 3.2%, 60: 4.1%, 146: 2.5%)

**The votes**: all four this run: scale, sharedcall, msc, opcall, jitdoes, jitrp, jitos, alignhot, jitret, jitvar over the last 3 runs in the ledger (77, 78, 79): candidates - scale (12 of 12), sharedcall (12 of 12), msc (12 of 12), alignhot (12 of 12); no keep vote and half the votes or more - none

**The rules** (rules.txt): 10 rules; 1 (The verdict) SEE, 3 (The fast gate) ATTENTION, 4 (The freeze) ATTENTION, 8 (tail) ATTENTION, 10 (Programs past 4 MB) SEE, the rest PASS

**The freezes lifted** (lifted.py): prediction 556 (the freezes were right): FAILS - bytehdr, thinhdr, bss, lean at 1 on 95%+: yes; alignhot, msc, sharedcall each on under 10%: no; doesfar and varcall at 1 everywhere: yes

**At 32 bits** (fail32.txt): at 32 bits: 27713 designs measured, 51 failed expected, no fault: 51 of 51 (cpt16's reach at 4-byte cells - Iteration 517); not a known cause: 0


**The deaths**: 51 wrong output: apps, 4 died: kernel workload, 312 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run. deaths-check.py rebuilt them here (design, generation, in the run, genes, here): none to rebuild.

**The chain on master**: process-checks.sh at 08ed7d6: build 0, run-tests PASS, validate 7 of 7 IDENTICAL, the gate alive 274 of 274, ans-check 0 of 66 differ (its negative control caught), deaths-check: 0 of 0 rebuilt compute right here; 0 open, the jail PASS.

**The verdict** (rule 1): no comparison step - the spread at generation 126 (session 66): seed 79 among the weakest of five there - the fastest 0.1435 (seeds 75-78: 0.1381-0.1477), the smallest 14,364 (the largest), 112 KB (the only one), the spread's hypervolume 0.6089 (the lowest); its gains came late. The three runs with the freezes lifted (77-79: 0.1418, 0.1477, 0.1435 at generation 126) are slower at the fast end than all three with the freezes on (74-76: 0.1381-0.1389) - 3 against 3 wholly apart, one-sided p = 0.05: suggestive, not conclusive.

**The stopping rule** (matched by generation): **seed 79 GAINS** - at its last generation, 177, against seed 77 at its end (161): the fastest 0.1378 against 0.1406 (-2.0%, at the threshold), the fastest at 32 bits 0.1643 against 0.1702 (-3.5%); the hypervolume +0.5%, the smallest +0.3%, the memory 112 KB against 108. The count: three runs, the earliest stop after seed 81. **But at generation 161 - seed 77's end, the exact match - seed 79 gains nothing**: the fastest 0.1395 (the bar 0.1378), 32-bit 0.1686 (0.1668), the hypervolume 0.35130 (0.35360); its gains came between generations 161 and 170, past seed 77's reach - seed 79 ran faster (177 generations in 12 hours against 161). The rule's documented lean (an earlier run shorter than this one is taken at its end - 562) decided the verdict; at equal effort the stop would have come after seed 79. The owner's choice (565).

**Prediction 563** (seed 79 gains nothing): **fails by the rule** - though it holds at equal effort (generation 161). **Prediction 556** (the freezes were right): **holds, as built** (lifted.py from the after-seed-79 batch: every lifted gene at its frozen value on the front as built).

**The confirmation run's founders** (carry-check.py --all, in the chain): 6,252 designs from 5 databases - seeds 75-79, the laptop's archives (71-74 no longer there) - 1.7 hours of generation 0; the named five all in (44aaae552d named, the rest on fronts); the sample 40 of 40 right against s6.

**The remote checks**: dream agrees as before (0.890, 32-bit 0.869, 64-bit 0.931); **the Tegra disagrees** - Spearman -0.103 over 16 (32-bit against the laptop's 0.441), where seed 78's check gave 0.926 with the same compiler (GCC 16.2.1): unexplained - the next chain's check will say whether it is the host.

**The deaths**: 4 on the kernel workload, every one cv8 at scale 1 with varcall=1 and doesfar=0 - the reach limit at scale 1 (Iteration 10; deaths-check.py names it since 564), one with the JIT on.

# Evolved VM designs

38963 designs evaluated, 26537 alive, 6456 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): dea923f5a8, speed 0.331, binary + image 23,495, memory 124 KB - Q 0.285 of s6-cv8b's.

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
